# Purchased human base: source evidence

Read-only Blender 5.0.1 audit, 2026-10-02. Counts exclude control widgets unless explicitly listed. FBX-imported `use_deform` flags are not reliable control/deform classifications; source .blend flags and skin membership are authoritative.

Source root: `C:/Users/Sez/Documents/Assets/rigged-stylized-human-body-base-mesh-rigged-3d-models`.

This appendix preserves the **initial 59-file snapshot**. The subsequently supplied 26 Boy/Girl shape-key files, their checksums and additional metadata are in [the child source update](CharacterBaseChildSourceUpdate.md); together the inventories cover all 85 current files.
